About

Hello,
my name is
Samuel.

Samuel is a photographer based in Madrid. His work is rooted in a documentary approach, capturing the real energy of each moment without interfering or staging.

Interested in what happens beyond the obvious — the gestures, the atmosphere, the in-between frames that define an event. Whether in concert halls or live venues, the goal is to translate the experience of being there into images that feel immediate and authentic.

contacto.samuelcobo@gmail.com linktr.ee/samusanjacobo

Showreel

Reel

Album

Orchestra

Live performance demands precision, awareness and timing. I document concerts with a discreet presence, focusing on expression, coordination and atmosphere. From full ensemble moments to individual musicians, my aim is to translate the intensity and precision of each show into still images. Every concert is approached with respect for the stage, the audience and the music itself. With experience working with large ensembles, I adapt confidently to low-light environments and provide complete visual coverage — from rehearsals to the live show.

Album

Live Music

Music is presence, rhythm and atmosphere. I document performances as they unfold, focusing on timing, expression and the relationship between artists, audience and space. Whether in concert halls or live venues, my goal is to translate the experience of being there into images that feel immediate and authentic. I capture live shows as they happen — reacting quickly to shifting lights, spontaneous interactions and the intensity of the stage. Each frame aims to preserve the raw connection between artist and audience.

Album

Street

Rooted in a documentary approach, capturing the real energy of each moment without interfering or staging. Interested in what happens beyond the obvious — the gestures, the atmosphere, the in-between frames that define a place. Street photography as a record of life unfolding, honest and immediate.

current = (n + total) % total; inner.style.transform = `translateX(-${current * 100}%)`; dots.forEach((d, i) => d.classList.toggle('active', i === current)); } // Arrow buttons const wrap = inner.closest('.slider-wrap'); wrap.querySelector('.slider-arrow.prev').addEventListener('click', () => goTo(current - 1)); wrap.querySelector('.slider-arrow.next').addEventListener('click', () => goTo(current + 1)); // Dot buttons dots.forEach((dot, i) => dot.addEventListener('click', () => goTo(i))); // Touch/swipe let touchStartX = 0; inner.addEventListener('touchstart', e => { touchStartX = e.touches[0].clientX; }, { passive: true }); inner.addEventListener('touchend', e => { const diff = touchStartX - e.changedTouches[0].clientX; if (Math.abs(diff) > 40) goTo(diff > 0 ? current + 1 : current - 1); }); }); // ── Subtle parallax on hero ── const heroBg = document.getElementById('heroBg'); window.addEventListener('scroll', () => { const y = window.scrollY; heroBg.style.transform = `translateY(${y * 0.4}px)`; }, { passive: true });
